================================================================================
FILE: backend/auth.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime, timedelta

import models
from database import get_db
from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from jose import JWTError, jwt
from passlib.context import CryptContext
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

SECRET_KEY = "vitals-vectors-dev-secret-change-in-production"
ALGORITHM = "HS256"
ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 60 * 24

pwd_context = CryptContext(schemes=["bcrypt"], deprecated="auto")
oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl="/auth/login")


def hash_password(password: str) -> str:
    return pwd_context.hash(password)


def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    return pwd_context.verify(plain_password, hashed_password)


def create_access_token(data: dict, expires_delta: timedelta | None = None) -> str:
    to_encode = data.copy()
    expire = datetime.utcnow() + (expires_delta or timedelta(minutes=ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES))
    to_encode.update({"exp": expire})
    return jwt.encode(to_encode, SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM)


def get_current_user(
    token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), db: Session = Depends(get_db)
) -> models.Member:
    credentials_exception = HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
        detail="Could not validate credentials",
        headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
    )
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM])
        member_id: str = payload.get("sub")
        if member_id is None:
            raise credentials_exception
    except JWTError:
        raise credentials_exception

    user = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == int(member_id)).first()
    if user is None:
        raise credentials_exception
    return user


def require_role(*roles: str):
    def checker(user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user)) -> models.Member:
        if user.role not in roles:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permissions",
            )
        return user

    return checker


================================================================================
FILE: backend/database.py
================================================================================

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import declarative_base, sessionmaker

SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URL = "sqlite:///./vitals_vectors.db"

engine = create_engine(
    SQLALCHEMY_DATABASE_URL, connect_args={"check_same_thread": False}
)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(autocommit=False, autoflush=False, bind=engine)
Base = declarative_base()


def get_db():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield db
    finally:
        db.close()

import models
from auth import hash_password
from database import Base, SessionLocal, engine
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware
from routes import admin as admin_routes
from routes import auth as auth_routes
from routes import leaderboard as leaderboard_routes
from routes import members as members_routes
from routes import points as points_routes
from routes import tasks as tasks_routes
from routes import warnings as warnings_routes

Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)

app = FastAPI(title="Vitals&Vectors API")

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=["*"],
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

app.include_router(auth_routes.router)

    return {"status": "Vitals&Vectors API online"}

def seed_admin():
    db = SessionLocal()
    try:
        existing = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.username == "admin").first()
        if existing:
            return
        admin = models.Member(
            name="Lab Lead",
            username="admin",
            password_hash=hash_password("admin123"),
            role="admin",
            points_total=100,
            rank="D",
            status="active",
        )
        db.add(admin)
        db.commit()
        print("Seeded default admin user -> username: admin / password: admin123")
    finally:
        db.close()


seed_admin()


================================================================================
FILE: backend/models.py

from datetime import datetime

from database import Base
from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    Column,
    DateTime,
    Float,
    ForeignKey,
    Integer,
    String,
)
from sqlalchemy.orm import relationship


class Member(Base):
    __tablename__ = "members"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    name = Column(String, nullable=False)
    username = Column(String, unique=True, index=True, nullable=False)
    password_hash = Column(String, nullable=False)
    role = Column(String, nullable=False, default="member")  # member / coordinator / admin

    points_total = Column(Integer, default=100)
    points_this_week = Column(Integer, default=0)
    points_this_month = Column(Integer, default=0)

    streak_presence = Column(Integer, default=0)
    streak_task_reporting = Column(Integer, default=0)

    warnings_count = Column(Integer, default=0)
    rank = Column(String, default="E")
    status = Column(String, default="active")  # active / suspended / disqualified
    joined_date = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    tasks_assigned = relationship(
        "Task", back_populates="assignee", foreign_keys="Task.assigned_to"
    )
    point_logs = relationship(
        "PointLog", back_populates="member", foreign_keys="PointLog.member_id"
    )
    warnings = relationship(
        "Warning", back_populates="member", foreign_keys="Warning.member_id"
    )


class Task(Base):
    __tablename__ = "tasks"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    title = Column(String, nullable=False)
    duration_hrs = Column(Float, default=0)
    deadline = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)
    priority = Column(Integer, default=2)  # 1 / 2 / 3

    assigned_to = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("members.id"), nullable=False)
    assigned_by = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("members.id"), nullable=False)

    status = Column(String, default="pending")  # pending/in_progress/completed/rated
    rating = Column(String, nullable=True)  # needs_revision/meets/exceeds
    points_awarded = Column(Integer, default=0)

    created_at = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    completed_at = Column(DateTime, nullable=True)

    assignee = relationship(
        "Member", back_populates="tasks_assigned", foreign_keys=[assigned_to]
    )


class PointLog(Base):
    __tablename__ = "point_logs"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    member_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("members.id"), nullable=False)
    event_type = Column(String, nullable=False)  # earn / deduct
    category = Column(String, nullable=False)  # attendance/task/learning/content/streak/conduct
    points = Column(Integer, nullable=False)
    reason = Column(String, default="")
    logged_by = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("members.id"), nullable=False)
    timestamp = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)

    member = relationship("Member", back_populates="point_logs", foreign_keys=[member_id])


class Warning(Base):
    __tablename__ = "warnings"

    id = Column(Integer, primary_key=True, index=True)
    member_id = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("members.id"), nullable=False)
    warning_number = Column(Integer, nullable=False)  # 1/2/3
    reason = Column(String, default="")
    issued_by = Column(Integer, ForeignKey("members.id"), nullable=False)
    date = Column(DateTime, default=datetime.utcnow)
    valid_reason_accepted = Column(Boolean, default=False)

    member = relationship("Member", back_populates="warnings", foreign_keys=[member_id])


================================================================================
FILE: backend/points_engine.py
================================================================================

import models
from ranking import rank_for_points
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session


def apply_points(
    db: Session,
    member: models.Member,
    event_type: str,
    category: str,
    points: int,
    reason: str,
    logged_by: int,
) -> models.PointLog:
    delta = points if event_type == "earn" else -points

    member.points_total += delta
    member.points_this_week += delta
    member.points_this_month += delta
    member.rank = rank_for_points(member.points_total)

    log = models.PointLog(
        member_id=member.id,
        event_type=event_type,
        category=category,
        points=points,
        reason=reason,
        logged_by=logged_by,
    )
    db.add(log)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(log)
    return log


================================================================================
FILE: backend/ranking.py
================================================================================

RANK_TABLE = [
    ("E", 0, 99, "Unranked Hunter"),
    ("D", 100, 149, "Bronze Hunter"),
    ("C", 150, 199, "Iron Hunter"),
    ("B", 200, 249, "Silver Hunter"),
    ("A", 250, 299, "Gold Hunter"),
    ("S", 300, 399, "Platinum Hunter"),
    ("SS", 400, None, "Shadow Monarch"),
]


def rank_for_points(points: int) -> str:
    for code, lo, hi, _label in RANK_TABLE:
        if hi is None:
            if points >= lo:
                return code
        elif lo <= points <= hi:
            return code
    return "E"


def rank_label(code: str) -> str:
    for c, _lo, _hi, label in RANK_TABLE:
        if c == code:
            return label
    return "Unranked Hunter"


def points_to_next_rank(points: int) -> int:
    current = rank_for_points(points)
    idx = next(i for i, r in enumerate(RANK_TABLE) if r[0] == current)
    if idx + 1 >= len(RANK_TABLE):
        return 0
    next_lo = RANK_TABLE[idx + 1][1]
    return max(next_lo - points, 0)

================================================================================
FILE: backend/routes/admin.py

from datetime import datetime

import models
import schemas
from auth import require_role
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["admin"])


@router.get("/alerts")
def alerts(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin", "coordinator")),
):
    low_points = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.points_total < 100).all()
    two_warnings = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.warnings_count == 2).all()
    overdue_tasks = (
        db.query(models.Task)
        .filter(models.Task.deadline < datetime.utcnow(), models.Task.status.in_(["pending", "in_progress"]))
        .all()
    )
    return {
        "low_points": [schemas.MemberOut.model_validate(m) for m in low_points],
        "two_warnings": [schemas.MemberOut.model_validate(m) for m in two_warnings],
        "overdue_tasks": [schemas.TaskOut.model_validate(t) for t in overdue_tasks],
    }


================================================================================
FILE: backend/routes/auth.py
================================================================================

import models
import schemas
from auth import create_access_token, verify_password
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth", tags=["auth"])


@router.post("/login", response_model=schemas.TokenResponse)
def login(payload: schemas.LoginRequest, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
    user = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.username == payload.username).first()
    if not user or not verify_password(payload.password, user.password_hash):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid username or password")
    if user.status != "active":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail=f"Account is {user.status}")

    token = create_access_token({"sub": str(user.id)})
    return schemas.TokenResponse(
        access_token=token, role=user.role, member_id=user.id, name=user.name
    )

import models
import schemas
from auth import get_current_user
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/leaderboard", tags=["leaderboard"])


@router.get("/weekly", response_model=list[schemas.MemberOut])
def weekly_leaderboard(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    return (
        db.query(models.Member)
        .filter(models.Member.status == "active")
        .order_by(models.Member.points_this_week.desc())
        .all()
    )


@router.get("/monthly", response_model=list[schemas.MemberOut])
def monthly_leaderboard(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    return (
        db.query(models.Member)
        .filter(models.Member.status == "active")
        .order_by(models.Member.points_this_month.desc())
        .all()
    )


@router.get("/hero-of-month", response_model=list[schemas.MemberOut])
def hero_of_month(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    return (
        db.query(models.Member)
        .filter(models.Member.status == "active", models.Member.points_this_month >= 300)
        .order_by(models.Member.points_this_month.desc())
        .all()
    )


================================================================================
FILE: backend/routes/members.py

import models
import schemas
from auth import get_current_user, hash_password, require_role
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/members", tags=["members"])


@router.get("", response_model=list[schemas.MemberOut])
def list_members(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if user.role == "member":
        return [db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == user.id).first()]
    return db.query(models.Member).order_by(models.Member.points_total.desc()).all()


@router.get("/me", response_model=schemas.MemberOut)
def get_me(user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user)):
    return user


@router.get("/{member_id}", response_model=schemas.MemberOut)
def get_member(
    member_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if user.role == "member" and user.id != member_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Cannot view other members")
    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == member_id).first()
    if not member:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not found")
    return member


@router.post("", response_model=schemas.MemberOut)
def create_member(
    payload: schemas.MemberCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin")),
):
    existing = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.username == payload.username).first()
    if existing:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Username already taken")

    member = models.Member(
        name=payload.name,
        username=payload.username,
        password_hash=hash_password(payload.password),
        role=payload.role,
        points_total=100,
        rank="D",
    )
    db.add(member)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(member)
    return member


@router.patch("/{member_id}", response_model=schemas.MemberOut)
def update_member(
    member_id: int,
    payload: schemas.MemberUpdate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin")),
):
    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == member_id).first()
    if not member:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not found")

    if payload.name is not None:
        member.name = payload.name
    if payload.role is not None:
        member.role = payload.role
    if payload.status is not None:
        member.status = payload.status

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(member)
    return member


@router.post("/{member_id}/deactivate", response_model=schemas.MemberOut)
def deactivate_member(
    member_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin")),
):
    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == member_id).first()
    if not member:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not found")
    member.status = "suspended"
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(member)
    return member


@router.post("/{member_id}/reset-password", response_model=schemas.MemberOut)
def reset_password(
    member_id: int,
    payload: schemas.PasswordReset,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin")),
):
    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == member_id).first()
    if not member:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not found")
    member.password_hash = hash_password(payload.new_password)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(member)
    return member


@router.get("/{member_id}/audit-log")
def member_audit_log(
    member_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin")),
):
    logs = (
        db.query(models.PointLog)
        .filter(models.PointLog.member_id == member_id)
        .order_by(models.PointLog.timestamp.desc())
        .all()
    )
    warnings = (
        db.query(models.Warning)
        .filter(models.Warning.member_id == member_id)
        .order_by(models.Warning.date.desc())
        .all()
    )
    return {
        "point_logs": [schemas.PointLogOut.model_validate(l) for l in logs],
        "warnings": [schemas.WarningOut.model_validate(w) for w in warnings],
    }

FILE: backend/routes/points.py

import models
import schemas
from auth import get_current_user, require_role
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from points_engine import apply_points
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/points", tags=["points"])


@router.post("", response_model=schemas.PointLogOut)
def log_points(
    payload: schemas.PointLogCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    actor: models.Member = Depends(require_role("coordinator", "admin")),
):
    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == payload.member_id).first()
    if not member:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not found")

    log = apply_points(
        db,
        member,
        event_type=payload.event_type,
        category=payload.category,
        points=payload.points,
        reason=payload.reason,
        logged_by=actor.id,
    )
    return log


@router.get("/member/{member_id}", response_model=list[schemas.PointLogOut])
def member_point_log(
    member_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if user.role == "member" and user.id != member_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Cannot view other members' logs")
    return (
        db.query(models.PointLog)
        .filter(models.PointLog.member_id == member_id)
        .order_by(models.PointLog.timestamp.desc())
        .all()
    )


================================================================================
FILE: backend/routes/tasks.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime

import models
import schemas
from auth import get_current_user, require_role
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from points_engine import apply_points
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/tasks", tags=["tasks"])

RATING_POINTS = {"needs_revision": 3, "meets": 5, "exceeds": 8}


@router.get("", response_model=list[schemas.TaskOut])
def list_tasks(
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if user.role == "member":
        return (
            db.query(models.Task)
            .filter(models.Task.assigned_to == user.id)
            .order_by(models.Task.deadline)
            .all()
        )
    return db.query(models.Task).order_by(models.Task.deadline).all()


@router.post("", response_model=schemas.TaskOut)
def create_task(
    payload: schemas.TaskCreateRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    actor: models.Member = Depends(require_role("coordinator", "admin")),
):
    assignee = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == payload.assigned_to).first()
    if not assignee:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Assignee not found")

    task = models.Task(
        title=payload.title,
        duration_hrs=payload.duration_hrs,
        deadline=payload.deadline,
        priority=payload.priority,
        assigned_to=payload.assigned_to,
        assigned_by=actor.id,
        status="pending",
    )
    db.add(task)
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(task)
    return task


@router.post("/{task_id}/complete", response_model=schemas.TaskOut)
def mark_complete(
    task_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    task = db.query(models.Task).filter(models.Task.id == task_id).first()
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Task not found")
    if user.role == "member" and task.assigned_to != user.id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Not your task")

    task.status = "completed"
    task.completed_at = datetime.utcnow()
    db.commit()
    db.refresh(task)
    return task


@router.post("/{task_id}/rate", response_model=schemas.TaskOut)
def rate_task(
    task_id: int,
    payload: schemas.TaskRateRequest,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    actor: models.Member = Depends(require_role("coordinator", "admin")),
):
    task = db.query(models.Task).filter(models.Task.id == task_id).first()
    if not task:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Task not found")
    if task.status != "completed":
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Task must be completed before rating")

    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == task.assigned_to).first()
    points = RATING_POINTS[payload.rating]

    task.rating = payload.rating
    task.points_awarded = points
    task.status = "rated"

    apply_points(
        db,
        member,
        event_type="earn",
        category="task",
        points=points,
        reason=f"Task rated '{payload.rating}': {task.title}",
        logged_by=actor.id,
    )

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(task)
    return task


================================================================================
FILE: backend/routes/warnings.py
================================================================================


import models
import schemas
from auth import get_current_user, require_role
from database import get_db
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

router = APIRouter(prefix="/warnings", tags=["warnings"])


@router.post("", response_model=schemas.WarningOut)
def issue_warning(
    payload: schemas.WarningCreate,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    admin: models.Member = Depends(require_role("admin")),
):
    member = db.query(models.Member).filter(models.Member.id == payload.member_id).first()
    if not member:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Member not found")

    member.warnings_count += 1
    warning = models.Warning(
        member_id=member.id,
        warning_number=member.warnings_count,
        reason=payload.reason,
        issued_by=admin.id,
        valid_reason_accepted=payload.valid_reason_accepted,
    )
    db.add(warning)

    if member.warnings_count >= 3:
        member.status = "suspended"

    db.commit()
    db.refresh(warning)
    return warning


@router.get("/member/{member_id}", response_model=list[schemas.WarningOut])
def member_warnings(
    member_id: int,
    db: Session = Depends(get_db),
    user: models.Member = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    if user.role == "member" and user.id != member_id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail="Cannot view other members' warnings")
    return (
        db.query(models.Warning)
        .filter(models.Warning.member_id == member_id)
        .order_by(models.Warning.date.desc())
        .all()
    )


================================================================================
FILE: backend/schemas.py
================================================================================

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel

Role = Literal["member", "coordinator", "admin"]
MemberStatus = Literal["active", "suspended", "disqualified"]
TaskStatus = Literal["pending", "in_progress", "completed", "rated"]
TaskRating = Literal["needs_revision", "meets", "exceeds"]
EventType = Literal["earn", "deduct"]
Category = Literal["attendance", "task", "learning", "content", "streak", "conduct"]


class LoginRequest(BaseModel):
    username: str
    password: str


class TokenResponse(BaseModel):
    access_token: str
    token_type: str = "bearer"
    role: Role
    member_id: int
    name: str


class MemberOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    name: str
    username: str
    role: Role
    points_total: int
    points_this_week: int
    points_this_month: int
    streak_presence: int
    streak_task_reporting: int
    warnings_count: int
    rank: str
    status: MemberStatus
    joined_date: datetime

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True


class MemberCreate(BaseModel):
    name: str
    username: str
    password: str
    role: Role = "member"


class MemberUpdate(BaseModel):
    name: str | None = None
    role: Role | None = None
    status: MemberStatus | None = None


class PasswordReset(BaseModel):
    new_password: str


class TaskCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    title: str
    duration_hrs: float = 0
    deadline: datetime | None = None
    priority: int = 2
    assigned_to: int


class TaskOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    title: str
    duration_hrs: float
    deadline: datetime | None
    priority: int
    assigned_to: int
    assigned_by: int
    status: TaskStatus
    rating: TaskRating | None
    points_awarded: int
    created_at: datetime
    completed_at: datetime | None

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True


class TaskRateRequest(BaseModel):
    rating: TaskRating


class PointLogCreate(BaseModel):
    member_id: int
    event_type: EventType
    category: Category
    points: int
    reason: str = ""


class PointLogOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    member_id: int
    event_type: EventType
    category: Category
    points: int
    reason: str
    logged_by: int
    timestamp: datetime

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True


class WarningCreate(BaseModel):
    member_id: int
    reason: str
    valid_reason_accepted: bool = False


class WarningOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    member_id: int
    warning_number: int
    reason: str
    issued_by: int
    date: datetime
    valid_reason_accepted: bool

    class Config:
        from_attributes = True

    // ─── TOAST (same pattern used across the app) ───
    function showToast(msg, type = 'info') {
      let c = document.getElementById('toast-container');
      if (!c) { c = document.createElement('div'); c.id = 'toast-container'; c.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:24px;right:24px;z-index:9999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;'; document.body.appendChild(c); }
      const colors = { success: '#00E5FF', error: '#FF1744', info: '#4FC3F7', warning: '#FFB300' };
      const t = document.createElement('div');
      t.style.cssText = `padding:12px 20px;border-radius:6px;font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:13px;border:1px solid ${colors[type]};color:${colors[type]};background:${colors[type]}18;max-width:340px;`;
      t.textContent = msg;
      c.appendChild(t);
      setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 3500);
    }

    // ─── SECTION 1: COMPUTED STATS ───
    function computeAlerts() {
      const belowMin = MEMBERS.filter(m => m.pts_month < 100);
      const twoWarns = MEMBERS.filter(m => m.warnings >= 2);
      const overdue = ALL_TASKS.filter(t => {
        if (t.status === 'rated' || t.status === 'completed') return false;
        return t.deadline < TODAY;
      });
      return { belowMin, twoWarns, overdue };
    }

    function renderStats() {
      const { belowMin, twoWarns, overdue } = computeAlerts();
      const elBelow = document.getElementById('statBelowMin');
      if (elBelow) elBelow.textContent = belowMin.length;
      const elWarns = document.getElementById('statTwoWarns');
      if (elWarns) elWarns.textContent = twoWarns.length;
      const elOverdue = document.getElementById('statOverdue');
      if (elOverdue) elOverdue.textContent = overdue.length;

      const totalAlerts = belowMin.length + twoWarns.length + overdue.length;
      const bannerCount = document.getElementById('alertBannerCount');
      if (bannerCount) bannerCount.textContent = totalAlerts;
      const banner = document.getElementById('alertBanner');
      if (banner) banner.style.display = totalAlerts > 0 ? 'flex' : 'none';
    }

    // ─── SECTION 2: TEAM OVERVIEW TABLE ───
    function renderTeamTable() {
      const sorted = [...MEMBERS].sort((a, b) => b.pts_month - a.pts_month);
      const body = document.getElementById('teamBody');
      body.innerHTML = sorted.map((m, i) => {
        const rc = RANK_COLORS[m.rank];
        const posStyle = i === 0 ? 'gold' : i === 1 ? 'silver' : i === 2 ? 'bronze' : 'plain';
        const statusChip = m.status === 'active'
          ? `<span class="chip-active">ACTIVE</span>`
          : `<span class="chip-warning">SUSPENDED</span>`;
        const warnDisplay = m.warnings > 0
          ? `<span style="color:${m.warnings >= 2 ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--amber)'}; font-weight:700;">${m.warnings} ⚠</span>`
          : `<span style="color:var(--text2)">—</span>`;

        return `
        <tr>
          <td><div class="rank-pos ${posStyle}">${i + 1}</div></td>
          <td>
            <div class="hunter-cell">
              <div class="h-avatar" style="background:${rc}18;border-color:${rc};color:${rc}">${m.init}</div>
              <a href="../hunter.html?name=${encodeURIComponent(m.name)}"
                 style="color:var(--text);text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;font-size:12.5px;"
                 onmouseover="this.style.color='var(--blue)'"
                 onmouseout="this.style.color='var(--text)'">${m.name}</a>
            </div>
          </td>
          <td class="td-pts">${m.pts_month}</td>
          <td class="td-week">+${m.pts_week}</td>
          <td class="td-streak">🔥 ${m.streak}w</td>
          <td>${statusChip}</td>
          <td>${warnDisplay}</td>
          <td>
            <div class="actions-cell">
              <button class="btn-pts-pos" onclick="openPoints('${m.name}','earn')">+ Pts</button>
              <button class="btn-pts-neg" onclick="openPoints('${m.name}','deduct')">− Pts</button>
              <button class="btn-warn" onclick="openWarn('${m.name}')">Warn</button>
            </div>
          </td>
        </tr>`;
      }).join('');
    }

    // ─── SECTION 3: ALERTS PANEL ───
    function renderAlerts() {
      const { belowMin, twoWarns, overdue } = computeAlerts();
      const list = document.getElementById('alertList');
      const items = [];

      twoWarns.forEach(m => {
        items.push({
          name: `${m.name} has ${m.warnings} warnings`,
          desc: 'Further violations may lead to suspension.',
          time: 'Active',
          color: 'var(--red)'
        });
      });

      belowMin.forEach(m => {
        items.push({
          name: `${m.name} is below 100 points (${m.pts_month} pts)`,
          desc: 'Encourage task completion and attendance.',
          time: 'Active',
          color: 'var(--amber)'
        });
      });

      if (overdue.length > 0) {
        items.push({
          name: `${overdue.length} task${overdue.length > 1 ? 's are' : ' is'} overdue`,
          desc: overdue.slice(0, 2).map(t => t.title).join(', ') + (overdue.length > 2 ? '...' : ''),
          time: 'Active',
          color: 'var(--blue)'
        });
      }

      if (items.length === 0) {
        list.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;color:var(--text2);padding:24px;font-size:13px;">No active alerts. All systems normal.</div>`;
        return;
      }

      list.innerHTML = items.map(item => `
        <div class="alert-item">
          <div class="alert-icon-wrap" style="border-color:${item.color}33;background:${item.color}10;">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="${item.color}" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
              <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"/><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"/>
            </svg>
          </div>
          <div class="alert-body">
            <div class="alert-title-row">
              <span class="alert-name">${item.name}</span>
              <span class="alert-time" style="color:${item.color}">${item.time}</span>
            </div>
            <div class="alert-desc">${item.desc}</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join('');
    }

    // ─── SECTION 4: HEROES PANEL ───
    function renderHeroes() {
      const heroWeek = [...MEMBERS].sort((a, b) => b.pts_week - a.pts_week)[0];
      document.getElementById('heroWeekName').textContent = heroWeek.name.toUpperCase();
      document.getElementById('heroWeekPts').textContent = `${heroWeek.pts_week} PTS THIS WEEK`;

      const heroMonth = MEMBERS.filter(m => m.pts_month >= 300).sort((a, b) => b.pts_month - a.pts_month);
      const hmList = document.getElementById('heroMonthList');
      if (heroMonth.length === 0) {
        hmList.innerHTML = `<div style="color:var(--text2);font-size:12px;padding:8px 0;">No members have reached 300 pts yet this month.</div>`;
      } else {
        hmList.innerHTML = heroMonth.map(m => `
          <div class="hm-row">
            <span class="hm-name">${m.name}</span>
            <span class="hm-pts">${m.pts_month} PTS</span>
          </div>
        `).join('');
      }
    }

    // ─── MODAL CONTROLLERS ───
    function openModal(modalId) {
      const backdrop = document.getElementById('modalBackdrop');
      document.querySelectorAll('.modal-card').forEach(m => m.classList.remove('active'));
      const target = document.getElementById(modalId);
      if (target) target.classList.add('active');
      backdrop.classList.add('active');
    }

    function closeAllModals() {
      const backdrop = document.getElementById('modalBackdrop');
      if (backdrop) backdrop.classList.remove('active');
      document.querySelectorAll('.modal-card').forEach(m => m.classList.remove('active'));
    }

    function handleBackdropClick(e) {
      if (e.target.id === 'modalBackdrop') {
        closeAllModals();
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') closeAllModals();
    });

    function openAddMemberModal() {
      document.getElementById('fAddName').value = '';
      document.getElementById('fAddUsername').value = '';
      document.getElementById('fAddPassword').value = '';
      document.getElementById('fAddRole').selectedIndex = 0;
      openModal('modalAddMember');
    }

    function openPointsModal(memberName, type = 'earn') {
      if (memberName) {
        const sel = document.getElementById('fMember');
        if (sel) sel.value = memberName;
      }
      const typeEl = document.getElementById('fType');
      if (typeEl) typeEl.value = type;
      openModal('modalPoints');
    }

    function openWarnModal(memberName) {
      if (memberName) {
        const sel = document.getElementById('fWarnMember');
        if (sel) sel.value = memberName;
      }
      openModal('modalWarn');
    }

    function toggleAddPw() {
      const pw = document.getElementById('fAddPassword');
      pw.type = pw.type === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password';
    }

    function populateMemberDropdowns() {
      const options = '<option value="" disabled selected>Select member</option>' +
        MEMBERS.map(m => `<option value="${m.name}">${m.name}</option>`).join('');
      ['#fMember', '#fWarnMember'].forEach(sel => {
        const el = document.querySelector(sel);
        if (el) el.innerHTML = options;
      });
    }

    function createHunterFromModal() {
      const name = document.getElementById('fAddName').value.trim();
      const username = document.getElementById('fAddUsername').value.trim();
      const role = document.getElementById('fAddRole').value;

      if (!name) {
        showToast('Please enter full name.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      const init = name.split(' ').map(n => n[0]).join('').toUpperCase().slice(0, 2) || 'HU';
      const newMember = {
        name: name,
        init: init,
        pts_week: 0,
        pts_month: 100,
        streak: 0,
        rank: 'd',
        warnings: 0,
        status: 'active'
      };

      MEMBERS.unshift(newMember);
      renderStats();
      renderTeamTable();
      renderAlerts();
      populateMemberDropdowns();
      closeAllModals();
      showToast(`Hunter "${name}" created successfully!`, 'success');
    }

    function logPoints() {
      const member = document.getElementById('fMember').value;
      const type = document.getElementById('fType').value;
      const category = document.getElementById('fCategory').value;
      const ptsVal = document.getElementById('fPoints').value;
      const reason = document.getElementById('fReason').value;

      if (!member || !type || !category || !ptsVal || !reason) {
        showToast('Please fill all fields before logging points.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      const pts = parseInt(ptsVal);
      const m = MEMBERS.find(x => x.name === member);
      if (m) {
        const delta = type === 'earn' ? Math.abs(pts) : -Math.abs(pts);
        m.pts_month = Math.max(0, m.pts_month + delta);
        if (delta > 0) m.pts_week += delta;
      }

      renderStats();
      renderTeamTable();
      renderAlerts();
      renderHeroes();
      closeAllModals();
      showToast(`Points logged for ${member} (${type === 'earn' ? '+' : '-'}${Math.abs(pts)} pts).`, 'success');
    }

    function issueWarning() {
      const member = document.getElementById('fWarnMember').value;
      const reason = document.getElementById('fWarnReason').value;
      if (!member || !reason) {
        showToast('Please select a member and enter a reason.', 'error');
        return;
      }

      const m = MEMBERS.find(x => x.name === member);
      if (m) {
        m.warnings += 1;
      }

      renderStats();
      renderTeamTable();
      renderAlerts();
      closeAllModals();
      showToast(`Warning issued to ${member}.`, 'warning');
    }

    function openPoints(memberName, type) {
      openPointsModal(memberName, type);
    }

    function openWarn(memberName) {
      openWarnModal(memberName);
    }

    // ─── SECTION 8: HERO CONFIRM BUTTONS ───
    function confirmHero(type) {
      if (type === 'week') {
        const hw = [...MEMBERS].sort((a, b) => b.pts_week - a.pts_week)[0];
        showToast(`Hero of Week confirmed: ${hw.name} (${hw.pts_week} pts). Will notify in next phase.`, 'success');
      } else {
        const hm = MEMBERS.filter(m => m.pts_month >= 300);
        if (hm.length === 0) {
          showToast('No members have reached 300 pts this month yet.', 'info');
        } else {
          showToast(`Hero of Month confirmed: ${hm.map(m => m.name).join(', ')}. Will notify in next phase.`, 'success');
        }
      }
    }

    document.getElementById('collapseBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
      sb.classList.toggle('collapsed');
      const ic = document.getElementById('collapseIcon');
      ic.innerHTML = sb.classList.contains('collapsed')
        ? '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/><polyline points="14 9 17 12 14 15"/>'
        : '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/><polyline points="16 15 13 12 16 9"/>';
    });

    function init() {
      renderStats();
      renderTeamTable();
      renderAlerts();
      renderHeroes();
      populateMemberDropdowns();
    }

    init();
  </script>
</body>

</html>

<script>

function openAssignModal() {
  document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.add('active');
}

function closeAssignModal() {
  document.getElementById('assignModal').classList.remove('active');
}

  { id:1,  projectId:'mindspace',   title:'Implement user onboarding flow',    lead:'Ganesh',    assignedTo:'Vedant',   duration:3,   deadline:'2026-08-20', priority:1, status:'in_progress', rating:null,          points:0  },

  { id:20, projectId:'website',     title:'Update laboratory documentation',    lead:'Debaditya', assignedTo:'Umesh',    duration:3,   deadline:'2026-08-22', priority:2, status:'pending',     rating:null,          points:0  },

function showToast(msg) {
  let c = document.getElementById('toast-container');
  if (!c) { c = document.createElement('div'); c.id = 'toast-container'; c.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:24px;right:24px;z-index:9999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;'; document.body.appendChild(c); }
  const t = document.createElement('div');
  t.style.cssText = `padding:12px 20px;border-radius:6px;font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:13px;border:1px solid #4FC3F7;color:#4FC3F7;background:rgba(79,195,247,0.1);animation:fadeIn 0.3s ease;max-width:340px;`;
  t.textContent = msg;
  c.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 3500);
}

let currentTab = 'all';
let searchQuery = '';
let activeQuests = [...ALL_TASKS];

function init() {
  const pSelect = document.getElementById('fProject');
  PROJECTS.forEach(p => {
    pSelect.innerHTML += `<option value="${p.id}">${p.name}</option>`;
  });
  renderQuests();
}

function onProjectSelect() {
  const pid = document.getElementById('fProject').value;
  const p = PROJECTS.find(x => x.id === pid);
  if(p) {
    document.getElementById('fLead').value = p.lead;
    const aSelect = document.getElementById('fAssignTo');
    aSelect.disabled = false;
    aSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected>Select member</option>' + p.members.map(m => `<option value="${m}">${m}</option>`).join('');
  }
}

function assignTask() {
  showToast("Task assignment will connect to backend in the next phase.");
  document.getElementById('fProject').selectedIndex = 0;
  document.getElementById('fLead').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fTitle').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fAssignTo').innerHTML = '<option value="" disabled selected>Select project first</option>';
  document.getElementById('fAssignTo').disabled = true;
  document.getElementById('fDuration').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fDeadline').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fPriority').selectedIndex = 0;
  closeAssignModal();
}

function switchTab(el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => {
    t.style.color = 'var(--text2)';
    t.style.borderBottomColor = 'transparent';
    t.style.background = 'transparent';
  });
  el.style.color = 'var(--blue)';
  el.style.borderBottomColor = 'var(--blue)';
  el.style.background = 'rgba(79,195,247,0.05)';
  currentTab = el.dataset.val;
  renderQuests();
}

function filterQuests(q) {
  searchQuery = q.toLowerCase();
  renderQuests();
}

function priorityChip(p) {
  if(p===1) return '<span class="chip chip-high">HIGH</span>';
  if(p===2) return '<span class="chip chip-medium">MEDIUM</span>';
  return '<span class="chip chip-low">LOW</span>';
}

function statusChip(s, r) {
  if(s==='in_progress') return '<span class="chip chip-progress">IN PROGRESS</span>';
  if(s==='pending')     return '<span class="chip chip-pending">PENDING</span>';
  if(s==='completed' && !r) return '<span class="chip chip-completed">COMPLETED</span>';
  if(s==='rated')       return '<span class="chip chip-rated" style="background:rgba(154,123,255,0.1);border-color:rgba(154,123,255,0.4);color:var(--purple);">RATED</span>';
  return '';
}

function rateCell(t) {
  if(t.status === 'completed' && !t.rating) {
    return `<select class="rate-select" onchange="handleRate(this, ${t.id})">
      <option value="">Rate task...</option>
      <option value="3">Needs revision (+3 pts)</option>
      <option value="5">Meets expectation (+5 pts)</option>
      <option value="8">Exceeds expectation (+8 pts)</option>
    </select>`;
  }
  if(t.rating) return `<span style="color:var(--purple); font-weight:bold;">${t.rating==='exceeds'?'Exceeds':t.rating==='meets'?'Meets':'Revision'}</span>`;
  return '<span class="rate-dash">—</span>';
}

function renderQuests() {
  let filtered = activeQuests.filter(t => {
    if(searchQuery && !t.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery) && !t.assignedTo.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery)) return false;
    if(currentTab === 'awaiting' && (t.status !== 'completed' || t.rating)) return false;
    if(currentTab === 'rated' && t.status !== 'rated') return false;
    return true;
  });
  
  const body = document.getElementById('questsBody');
  body.innerHTML = filtered.map((q, i) => {
    const p = PROJECTS.find(pr => pr.id === q.projectId);
    return `<tr>
      <td>${i+1}</td>
      <td class="td-title">${q.title}</td>
      <td><div class="assignee-cell">
        <div class="h-avatar" style="border-color:${p.color};color:${p.color};background:${p.color}18">${q.assignedTo.charAt(0)}</div>
        <span class="h-name">${q.assignedTo}</span>
      </div></td>
      <td class="td-deadline">${new Date(q.deadline).toLocaleDateString('en-GB')}</td>
      <td>${priorityChip(q.priority)}</td>
      <td>${statusChip(q.status, q.rating)}</td>
      <td>${rateCell(q)}</td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
  
  document.getElementById('pgInfo').textContent = `Showing 1 to ${filtered.length} of ${filtered.length} quests`;
}

function handleRate(sel, id) {
  if(!sel.value) return;
  const task = activeQuests.find(t => t.id === id);
  if(task) {
    task.rating = sel.value === '8' ? 'exceeds' : sel.value === '5' ? 'meets' : 'needs_revision';
    task.points = parseInt(sel.value);
    task.status = 'rated';
  }
  showToast("Rating will be saved to backend in the next phase.");
  renderQuests();
}

init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

              <option value="AI/ML">AI / ML</option>

          <div class="select-wrap">

              <option value="e">E Rank (Unranked)</option>
              <option value="d">D Rank (Bronze)</option>
              <option value="c" selected>C Rank (Iron)</option>
              <option value="b">B Rank (Silver)</option>

<!-- TOAST -->
<div class="toast-notif" id="toastNotif">
  <div class="toast-icon">
    <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 11-5.93-9.14"/><polyline points="22 4 12 14.01 9 11.01"/></svg>
  </div>
  <span id="toastMsg">Action completed successfully.</span>
</div>

<script>
const RANK_COLORS = { e:'#8A93A8', d:'#CD7F32', c:'#B0B0B0', b:'#4FC3F7', a:'#FFD700', s:'#00E5FF', ss:'#9A7BFF' };

let HUNTERS = [
  { initials:'GA', name:'Ganesh',    username:'ganesh@vitals.lab',    domain:'Full Stack', role:'coordinator', rank:'s',  pts:16000, status:'active' },
  { initials:'DE', name:'Debaditya', username:'debaditya@vitals.lab', domain:'AI/ML',      role:'coordinator', rank:'a',  pts:14900, status:'active' },
  { initials:'DW', name:'Deepavali', username:'deepavali@vitals.lab', domain:'UI/UX',      role:'coordinator', rank:'a',  pts:13000, status:'active' },
  { initials:'NA', name:'Nandini',   username:'nandini@vitals.lab',   domain:'Content',    role:'member',      rank:'b',  pts:12250, status:'active' },
  { initials:'SW', name:'Swapnil',   username:'swapnil@vitals.lab',   domain:'Full Stack', role:'member',      rank:'b',  pts:11000, status:'active' },
  { initials:'SU', name:'Suraj',     username:'suraj@vitals.lab',     domain:'Operations', role:'member',      rank:'b',  pts:10500, status:'active' },
  { initials:'SA', name:'Santosh',   username:'santosh@vitals.lab',   domain:'AI/ML',      role:'member',      rank:'c',  pts:9750,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'NK', name:'Nikita',    username:'nikita@vitals.lab',    domain:'UI/UX',      role:'member',      rank:'c',  pts:9250,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'VE', name:'Vedant',    username:'vedant@vitals.lab',    domain:'Full Stack', role:'member',      rank:'c',  pts:8500,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'NC', name:'Nakul',     username:'nakul@vitals.lab',     domain:'Content',    role:'member',      rank:'c',  pts:7750,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'AS', name:'Ashutosh',  username:'ashutosh@vitals.lab',  domain:'Operations', role:'member',      rank:'d',  pts:7000,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'VS', name:'Vishal',    username:'vishal@vitals.lab',    domain:'AI/ML',      role:'member',      rank:'d',  pts:6500,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'KO', name:'Komal',     username:'komal@vitals.lab',     domain:'UI/UX',      role:'member',      rank:'d',  pts:5900,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'PR', name:'Prerna',    username:'prerna@vitals.lab',    domain:'Full Stack', role:'member',      rank:'d',  pts:5500,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'SH', name:'Shreya',    username:'shreya@vitals.lab',    domain:'Content',    role:'member',      rank:'d',  pts:5000,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'PM', name:'Prem',      username:'prem@vitals.lab',      domain:'Operations', role:'member',      rank:'e',  pts:4400,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'KR', name:'Krishna',   username:'krishna@vitals.lab',   domain:'AI/ML',      role:'member',      rank:'e',  pts:3600,  status:'active' },
  { initials:'UM', name:'Umesh',     username:'umesh@vitals.lab',     domain:'UI/UX',      role:'member',      rank:'e',  pts:2250,  status:'active' },
];

let currentPage = 1;
const pageSize = 10;

function domainClass(d) {
  if (d === 'AI/ML') return 'domain-aiml';
  if (d === 'Full Stack') return 'domain-fullstack';
  if (d === 'UI/UX') return 'domain-uiux';
  if (d === 'Content') return 'domain-content';
  return 'domain-ops';
}

function roleLabel(r) {
  if (r === 'coordinator') return '<span class="role-chip role-coordinator">U5 COORDINATOR</span>';
  if (r === 'admin')       return '<span class="role-chip role-admin">ADMIN</span>';
  return                          '<span class="role-chip role-member">MEMBER</span>';
}

function rankHex(r) {
  return `<div class="rank-hex rank-${r}">${r.toUpperCase()}</div>`;
}

function statusChip(s) {
  return s === 'active'
    ? '<span class="chip-active">ACTIVE</span>'
    : '<span class="chip-inactive">INACTIVE</span>';
}

function getFilteredHunters() {
  const q = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.toLowerCase().trim();
  const rf = document.getElementById('roleFilter').value;
  const df = document.getElementById('domainFilter').value;
  const rkf = document.getElementById('rankFilter').value;

  return HUNTERS.filter(h => {
    const matchQ = !q || h.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || h.username.toLowerCase().includes(q) || h.domain.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    const matchRole = rf === 'all' || h.role === rf;
    const matchDomain = df === 'all' || h.domain === df;
    const matchRank = rkf === 'all' || h.rank === rkf;
    return matchQ && matchRole && matchDomain && matchRank;
  });
}

function updateKPIs() {
  document.getElementById('kpiTotal').textContent = HUNTERS.length;
  document.getElementById('kpiActive').textContent = HUNTERS.filter(h => h.status === 'active').length;
  document.getElementById('kpiCoords').textContent = HUNTERS.filter(h => h.role === 'coordinator' || h.role === 'admin').length;
  const totalPts = HUNTERS.reduce((acc, h) => acc + h.pts, 0);
  document.getElementById('kpiPoints').textContent = totalPts.toLocaleString();
}

function renderTable() {
  const filtered = getFilteredHunters();
  const total = filtered.length;
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(total / pageSize) || 1;
  if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages;

  const startIdx = (currentPage - 1) * pageSize;
  const pageData = filtered.slice(startIdx, startIdx + pageSize);

  const body = document.getElementById('huntersBody');
  const rc = RANK_COLORS;

  if (pageData.length === 0) {
    body.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="9" style="text-align:center;padding:28px;color:var(--text2);">No hunters found matching your filters.</td></tr>`;
  } else {
    body.innerHTML = pageData.map((h, i) => {
      const globalIdx = startIdx + i + 1;
      const c = rc[h.rank] || '#8A93A8';
      return `<tr>
        <td style="color:var(--text2);font-weight:600;">${globalIdx}</td>
        <td>
          <div class="name-cell">
            <div class="h-avatar" style="border-color:${c};color:${c};background:${c}18">${h.initials}</div>
            <div class="h-info">
              <span class="h-name">${h.name}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td class="h-email">${h.username}</td>
        <td><span class="domain-badge ${domainClass(h.domain)}">${h.domain}</span></td>
        <td>${roleLabel(h.role)}</td>
        <td>${rankHex(h.rank)}</td>
        <td class="td-pts">${h.pts.toLocaleString()}</td>
        <td>${statusChip(h.status)}</td>
        <td>
          <div class="actions-cell">
            <button class="btn-edit" onclick="editHunter('${h.username}')">Edit</button>
            <button class="btn-deactivate" onclick="toggleStatus('${h.username}')">${h.status === 'active' ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}</button>
          </div>
        </td>
      </tr>`;
    }).join('');
  }

  // Pagination info
  const endIdx = Math.min(startIdx + pageSize, total);
  document.getElementById('pgInfo').textContent = total > 0 ? `Showing ${startIdx + 1} to ${endIdx} of ${total} hunters` : '0 hunters';

  // Pagination buttons
  let pgHtml = '';
  for (let p = 1; p <= totalPages; p++) {
    pgHtml += `<button class="pg-btn ${p === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setPage(${p})">${p}</button>`;
  }
  document.getElementById('pgNumbers').innerHTML = pgHtml;

  updateKPIs();
}

function filterTable() {
  currentPage = 1;
  renderTable();
}

function setPage(p) {
  currentPage = p;
  renderTable();
}

function prevPage() {
  if (currentPage > 1) { currentPage--; renderTable(); }
}

function nextPage() {
  const filtered = getFilteredHunters();
  const totalPages = Math.ceil(filtered.length / pageSize) || 1;
  if (currentPage < totalPages) { currentPage++; renderTable(); }
}

function toggleStatus(username) {
  const h = HUNTERS.find(x => x.username === username);
  if (h) {
    h.status = h.status === 'active' ? 'inactive' : 'active';
    renderTable();
    showToast(`${h.name} is now ${h.status.toUpperCase()}.`);
  }
}

function editHunter(username) {
  const h = HUNTERS.find(x => x.username === username);
  if (h) {
    document.getElementById('fName').value = h.name;
    document.getElementById('fUsername').value = h.username;
    document.getElementById('fRole').value = h.role;
    document.getElementById('fDomain').value = h.domain;
    document.getElementById('fRank').value = h.rank;
    document.getElementById('fPoints').value = h.pts;
    openAddMemberModal();
  }
}

/* ── MODAL POPUP LOGIC ── */
function openAddMemberModal() {
  document.getElementById('modalBackdrop').classList.add('active');
  document.getElementById('modalAddMember').classList.add('active');
}

function closeModalAddMember() {
  document.getElementById('modalBackdrop').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('modalAddMember').classList.remove('active');
}

function handleBackdropClick(e) {
  if (e.target === document.getElementById('modalBackdrop')) {
    closeModalAddMember();
  }
}
document.getElementById('modalBackdrop').addEventListener('click', handleBackdropClick);

document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModalAddMember();
});

function togglePw() {
  const pw = document.getElementById('fPassword');
  pw.type = pw.type === 'password' ? 'text' : 'password';
}

function submitCreateMember() {
  const name = document.getElementById('fName').value.trim();
  const username = document.getElementById('fUsername').value.trim();
  const role = document.getElementById('fRole').value;
  const domain = document.getElementById('fDomain').value;
  const rank = document.getElementById('fRank').value;
  const pts = parseInt(document.getElementById('fPoints').value) || 0;

  if (!name) {
    alert('Please enter a full name for the new member.');
    return;
  }

  const nameParts = name.split(' ');
  const initials = nameParts.length >= 2 ? (nameParts[0][0] + nameParts[1][0]).toUpperCase() : name.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase();
  const userEmail = username ? (username.includes('@') ? username : `${username}@vitals.lab`) : `${name.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '.')}@vitals.lab`;

  // Check if exists
  const existingIdx = HUNTERS.findIndex(h => h.username === userEmail || h.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());
  if (existingIdx !== -1) {
    HUNTERS[existingIdx] = { initials, name, username: userEmail, domain, role, rank, pts, status: 'active' };
    showToast(`Updated member "${name}".`);
  } else {
    HUNTERS.unshift({ initials, name, username: userEmail, domain, role, rank, pts, status: 'active' });
    showToast(`Member "${name}" created successfully.`);
  }

  closeModalAddMember();
  // reset form
  document.getElementById('fName').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fUsername').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fPassword').value = '';
  document.getElementById('fPoints').value = '0';

  filterTable();
}

function exportCSV() {
  const headers = ["Index", "Name", "Email", "Domain", "Role", "Rank", "Points", "Status"];
  const rows = HUNTERS.map((h, i) => [i + 1, `"${h.name}"`, `"${h.username}"`, `"${h.domain}"`, `"${h.role}"`, `"${h.rank.toUpperCase()}"`, h.pts, h.status]);
  const csvContent = "data:text/csv;charset=utf-8," + [headers.join(","), ...rows.map(e => e.join(","))].join("\n");
  const encodedUri = encodeURI(csvContent);
  const link = document.createElement("a");
  link.setAttribute("href", encodedUri);
  link.setAttribute("download", "Vitals_Vectors_Hunters.csv");
  document.body.appendChild(link);
  link.click();
  document.body.removeChild(link);
  showToast("Exported CSV file successfully.");
}

function showToast(msg) {
  const toast = document.getElementById('toastNotif');
  document.getElementById('toastMsg').textContent = msg;
  toast.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 3000);
}

// Auto open modal if URL params indicate action=add-member
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  renderTable();
  if (window.location.search.includes('action=add-member') || window.location.search.includes('openModal=add-member')) {
    openAddMemberModal();
  }
});

document.getElementById('collapseBtn').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  sb.classList.toggle('collapsed');
  const ic = document.getElementById('collapseIcon');
  ic.innerHTML = sb.classList.contains('collapsed')
    ? '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/><polyline points="14 9 17 12 14 15"/>'
    : '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/><polyline points="16 15 13 12 16 9"/>';
});
</script>
</body>
</html>

document.getElementById('collapseBtn')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
  const sb = document.getElementById('sidebar');
  const ic = document.getElementById('collapseIcon');
  sb.classList.toggle('collapsed');
  if (sb.classList.contains('collapsed')) {
    ic.innerHTML = '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/><polyline points="14 9 17 12 14 15"/>';
  } else {
    ic.innerHTML = '<rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><line x1="9" y1="3" x2="9" y2="21"/><polyline points="16 15 13 12 16 9"/>';
  }
});

function showToast(msg, type = 'info') {
  let c = document.getElementById('toast-container');
  if (!c) { c = document.createElement('div'); c.id = 'toast-container'; c.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:24px;right:24px;z-index:9999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;'; document.body.appendChild(c); }
  const colors = { success:'#00E5FF', error:'#FF1744', info:'#4FC3F7', warning:'#FFB300' };
  const t = document.createElement('div');
  t.style.cssText = `padding:12px 20px;border-radius:6px;font-family:'Inter',sans-serif;font-size:13px;border:1px solid ${colors[type]};color:${colors[type]};background:${colors[type]}18;animation:fadeIn 0.3s ease;max-width:340px;`;
  t.textContent = msg;
  c.appendChild(t);
  setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 3500);
}

const TODAY = '2026-08-16';

const ASSIGNEES = [...new Set(ALL_TASKS.map(t => t.assignedTo))].sort();

const TABS = [
  { id:'all',       label:'All Tasks' },
  { id:'today',     label:"Today's Tasks" },
  { id:'yesterday', label:"Yesterday's Tasks" },
  { id:'week',      label:'This Week' },
  { id:'completed', label:'Completed' },
  { id:'overdue',   label:'Overdue' },
];

function addDays(dateStr, n) {
  const d = new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00');
  d.setDate(d.getDate() + n);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
}

const YESTERDAY = addDays(TODAY, -1);
const WEEK_START = (() => {
  const d = new Date(TODAY + 'T00:00:00');
  const dow = (d.getDay() + 6) % 7; // Mon=0
  d.setDate(d.getDate() - dow);
  return d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
})();
const WEEK_END = addDays(WEEK_START, 6);

function isOpen(t) { return t.status !== 'completed' && t.status !== 'rated'; }

const TAB_PREDICATES = {
  all:       t => true,
  today:     t => t.deadline === TODAY,
  yesterday: t => t.deadline === YESTERDAY,
  week:      t => t.deadline >= WEEK_START && t.deadline <= WEEK_END,
  completed: t => t.status === 'completed' || t.status === 'rated',
  overdue:   t => isOpen(t) && t.deadline < TODAY,
};

let activeTab = 'all';
let searchText = '';
let filters = { status: [], priority: [], projectIds: [], assignees: [], dateFrom: '', dateTo: '' };
let currentPage = 1;
let PER_PAGE = 10;
let filteredTasks = ALL_TASKS;

function srNumber(id) {
  return 'SR-2026-' + String(100 + id).padStart(5, '0');
}

function priorityChip(p) {
  if (p === 1) return `<span class="chip chip-high">High</span>`;
  if (p === 2) return `<span class="chip chip-medium">Medium</span>`;
  return `<span class="chip chip-low">Low</span>`;
}
function priorityLabel(p) { return p === 1 ? 'High' : p === 2 ? 'Medium' : 'Low'; }

function statusChip(t) {
  if (t.status === 'pending') return `<span class="chip chip-pending">Pending</span>`;
  if (t.status === 'in_progress') return `<span class="chip chip-progress">In Progress</span>`;
  if (t.status === 'completed' && !t.rating) return `<span class="chip chip-completed">Completed</span>`;
  if (t.status === 'rated') return `<span class="chip chip-rated">Rated</span>`;
  return '';
}
function statusLabel(t) {
  if (t.status === 'pending') return 'Pending';
  if (t.status === 'in_progress') return 'In Progress';
  if (t.status === 'completed' && !t.rating) return 'Completed';
  if (t.status === 'rated') return 'Rated';
  return t.status;
}

function fmtDate(dateStr) {
  return new Date(dateStr + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString('en-US', { day:'numeric', month:'short', year:'numeric' });
}

function renderTabs() {
  document.getElementById('tabBar').innerHTML = TABS.map(tb =>
    `<div class="tab ${tb.id === activeTab ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="setTab('${tb.id}')">${tb.label}</div>`
  ).join('');
}

function setTab(id) {
  activeTab = id;
  currentPage = 1;
  renderTabs();
  updateTable();
}

function onSearchInput(v) {
  searchText = v.trim().toLowerCase();
  currentPage = 1;
  updateTable();
}

function setPageSize(v) {
  PER_PAGE = parseInt(v, 10);
  currentPage = 1;
  renderTable();
}

/* ---------- filter modal ---------- */

function buildFilterModalOptions() {
  const statusDefs = [
    { v:'pending', l:'Pending' }, { v:'in_progress', l:'In Progress' },
    { v:'completed', l:'Completed' }, { v:'rated', l:'Rated' },
  ];
  document.getElementById('fStatus').innerHTML = statusDefs.map(s =>
    `<label class="filter-check ${filters.status.includes(s.v) ? 'checked' : ''}">
      <input type="checkbox" value="${s.v}" ${filters.status.includes(s.v) ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleFilter('status','${s.v}',this)">${s.l}
    </label>`).join('');

  const prioDefs = [{v:1,l:'High'},{v:2,l:'Medium'},{v:3,l:'Low'}];
  document.getElementById('fPriority').innerHTML = prioDefs.map(p =>
    `<label class="filter-check ${filters.priority.includes(p.v) ? 'checked' : ''}">
      <input type="checkbox" value="${p.v}" ${filters.priority.includes(p.v) ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleFilter('priority',${p.v},this)">${p.l}
    </label>`).join('');

  document.getElementById('fProject').innerHTML = PROJECTS.map(p =>
    `<label class="filter-check ${filters.projectIds.includes(p.id) ? 'checked' : ''}">
      <input type="checkbox" value="${p.id}" ${filters.projectIds.includes(p.id) ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleFilter('projectIds','${p.id}',this)">${p.name}
    </label>`).join('');

  document.getElementById('fAssignee').innerHTML = ASSIGNEES.map(a =>
    `<label class="filter-check ${filters.assignees.includes(a) ? 'checked' : ''}">
      <input type="checkbox" value="${a}" ${filters.assignees.includes(a) ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="toggleFilter('assignees','${a}',this)">${a}
    </label>`).join('');

  document.getElementById('fDateFrom').value = filters.dateFrom;
  document.getElementById('fDateTo').value = filters.dateTo;
}

function toggleFilter(group, value, el) {
  const arr = filters[group];
  const idx = arr.indexOf(value);
  if (idx === -1) arr.push(value); else arr.splice(idx, 1);
  el.closest('.filter-check').classList.toggle('checked', idx === -1);
}

function openFilterModal() {
  buildFilterModalOptions();
  document.getElementById('filterOverlay').classList.add('open');
}
function closeFilterModal() {
  document.getElementById('filterOverlay').classList.remove('open');
}
function applyFilterModal() {
  filters.dateFrom = document.getElementById('fDateFrom').value;
  filters.dateTo = document.getElementById('fDateTo').value;
  currentPage = 1;
  closeFilterModal();
  updateTable();
}
function clearFilterModal() {
  filters = { status: [], priority: [], projectIds: [], assignees: [], dateFrom: '', dateTo: '' };
  buildFilterModalOptions();
}

function activeFilterCount() {
  return filters.status.length + filters.priority.length + filters.projectIds.length + filters.assignees.length
    + (filters.dateFrom ? 1 : 0) + (filters.dateTo ? 1 : 0);
}

function updateFilterBadge() {
  const n = activeFilterCount();
  const badge = document.getElementById('filterBadge');
  badge.textContent = n;
  badge.style.display = n > 0 ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
}

/* ---------- filtering + render ---------- */

function getFilteredTasks() {
  return ALL_TASKS.filter(t => {
    if (!TAB_PREDICATES[activeTab](t)) return false;

    if (searchText) {
      const p = PROJECTS.find(pr => pr.id === t.projectId);
      const hay = `${t.title} ${p ? p.name : ''} ${t.assignedTo} ${t.lead}`.toLowerCase();
      if (!hay.includes(searchText)) return false;
    }

    if (filters.status.length && !filters.status.includes(t.status)) return false;
    if (filters.priority.length && !filters.priority.includes(t.priority)) return false;
    if (filters.projectIds.length && !filters.projectIds.includes(t.projectId)) return false;
    if (filters.assignees.length && !filters.assignees.includes(t.assignedTo)) return false;
    if (filters.dateFrom && t.deadline < filters.dateFrom) return false;
    if (filters.dateTo && t.deadline > filters.dateTo) return false;

    return true;
  });
}

function updateTable() {
  filteredTasks = getFilteredTasks();
  updateFilterBadge();
  renderTable();
}

function renderTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('taskBody');
  const pgInfo = document.getElementById('pgInfo');
  const pgC = document.getElementById('pgControls');

  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredTasks.length / PER_PAGE));
  if (currentPage > totalPages) currentPage = totalPages;
  const start = filteredTasks.length ? (currentPage - 1) * PER_PAGE : 0;
  const end = Math.min(start + PER_PAGE, filteredTasks.length);
  const pageTasks = filteredTasks.slice(start, end);

  pgInfo.innerText = `Showing ${filteredTasks.length ? start + 1 : 0} to ${end} of ${filteredTasks.length} tasks`;

  let pgHtml = `<button class="pg-btn" onclick="setPage(${currentPage-1})" ${currentPage===1?'disabled':''}>&lt;</button>`;
  for (let i = 1; i <= totalPages; i++) {
    pgHtml += `<button class="pg-btn ${i===currentPage?'active':''}" onclick="setPage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
  }
  pgHtml += `<button class="pg-btn" onclick="setPage(${currentPage+1})" ${currentPage===totalPages?'disabled':''}>&gt;</button>`;
  pgC.innerHTML = pgHtml;

  if (!pageTasks.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="10" style="text-align:center; padding:32px; color:var(--text2);">No tasks match the current view.</td></tr>`;
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = pageTasks.map((t, idx) => {
    const p = PROJECTS.find(pr => pr.id === t.projectId);
    const isPast = isOpen(t) && t.deadline < TODAY;
    const dlColor = isPast ? 'var(--red)' : 'var(--text2)';

    return `<tr>
      <td>${start + idx + 1}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--text2); font-size:12px;">${srNumber(t.id)}</td>
      <td><div class="pill-proj" style="background:${p.color}26; color:${p.color}; border-color:${p.color}" onclick="window.location.href='project.html?id=${p.id}'">${p.name}</div></td>
      <td><div style="font-weight:bold;color:white;">${t.title}</div><div style="font-size:11px;color:var(--text2)">${t.duration}h</div></td>
      <td style="color:var(--text2)">${t.lead}</td>
      <td>
        <div class="avatar-chip" onclick="window.location.href='hunter.html?name=' + encodeURIComponent('${t.assignedTo}')">
          <div class="avatar-circle" style="background:${p.color}33; border-color:${p.color}; color:${p.color}">${t.assignedTo.charAt(0)}</div>
          ${t.assignedTo}
        </div>
      </td>
      <td style="color:${dlColor}">${fmtDate(t.deadline)}</td>
      <td>${statusChip(t)}</td>
      <td>${priorityChip(t.priority)}</td>
      <td>
        <div class="actions-cell">
          <button class="action-icon-btn" title="View" onclick="openTaskPopover(event, ${t.id})">
            <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M1 12s4-8 11-8 11 8 11 8-4 8-11 8-11-8-11-8z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/></svg>
          </button>
          <button class="action-icon-btn" title="More" onclick="taskActionsMenu(${t.id})">
            <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><circle cx="12" cy="5" r="1.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="1.5"/><circle cx="12" cy="19" r="1.5"/></svg>
          </button>
        </div>
      </td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}

function setPage(p) {
  const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filteredTasks.length / PER_PAGE));
  if (p < 1 || p > totalPages) return;
  currentPage = p;
  renderTable();
}

/* ---------- task popover + actions ---------- */

function closeTaskPopover() {
  document.getElementById('taskPopoverEl')?.remove();
  document.removeEventListener('click', popoverOutsideClick, true);
}
function popoverOutsideClick(e) {
  const el = document.getElementById('taskPopoverEl');
  if (el && !el.contains(e.target)) closeTaskPopover();
}

function openTaskPopover(evt, id) {
  evt.stopPropagation();
  closeTaskPopover();
  const t = ALL_TASKS.find(x => x.id === id);
  const p = PROJECTS.find(pr => pr.id === t.projectId);
  const rect = evt.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();

  const el = document.createElement('div');
  el.className = 'task-popover';
  el.id = 'taskPopoverEl';
  el.style.top = Math.min(rect.bottom + 8, window.innerHeight - 280) + 'px';
  el.style.left = Math.min(rect.left - 240, window.innerWidth - 300) + 'px';
  el.innerHTML = `
    <div class="tp-title">${t.title}</div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">SR Number</span><span class="tp-val">${srNumber(t.id)}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Project</span><span class="tp-val" style="color:${p.color}">${p.name}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Assigned by</span><span class="tp-val">${t.lead}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Assigned to</span><span class="tp-val">${t.assignedTo}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Deadline</span><span class="tp-val">${fmtDate(t.deadline)}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Priority</span><span class="tp-val">${priorityLabel(t.priority)}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Status</span><span class="tp-val">${statusLabel(t)}</span></div>
    <div class="tp-row"><span class="tp-label">Points</span><span class="tp-val">${t.points || '—'}</span></div>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  setTimeout(() => document.addEventListener('click', popoverOutsideClick, true), 0);
}

function taskActionsMenu(id) {
  showToast('Task actions coming soon', 'info');
}

/* ---------- CSV export ---------- */

function csvEscape(v) {
  const s = String(v ?? '');
  return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s;
}

function exportCSV() {
  const rows = [
    ['SR Number', 'Project', 'Task', 'Assigned By', 'Assigned To', 'Deadline', 'Status', 'Priority'],
    ...filteredTasks.map(t => {
      const p = PROJECTS.find(pr => pr.id === t.projectId);
      return [srNumber(t.id), p.name, t.title, t.lead, t.assignedTo, t.deadline, statusLabel(t), priorityLabel(t.priority)];
    }),
  ];
  const csv = rows.map(r => r.map(csvEscape).join(',')).join('\n');
  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = `all-tasks-${TODAY}.csv`;
  document.body.appendChild(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  showToast(`Exported ${filteredTasks.length} tasks`, 'success');
}

function init() {
  renderTabs();
  updateTable();
}

init();
</script>
</body>
</html>

// ── MEMBER DATA ──────────────────────────────────────

const ATTENDANCE_DATA = [
  { date:'2026-08-15', checkIn:'10:21', checkOut:'--:--', duration:'00h 00m', override:false },
  { date:'2026-08-14', checkIn:'10:27', checkOut:'--:--', duration:'00h 00m', override:false },
  { date:'2026-08-13', checkIn:'09:49', checkOut:'23:45', duration:'13h 55m', override:false },
